# Tantrāloka: verse-page map (KSTS)

This note gives, for every verse of the Tantrāloka (5,845 verse notes), the page where it starts in the Kashmir Series edition. Each link opens the volume's PDF in the vault at that page. The same data, one row per verse, is in [[Library/Sanskrit/KSTS/Trika/KSTS - Tantraloka Sanskrit (vols I-XII)/tantraloka-pages.tsv|tantraloka-pages.tsv]]. Details of the volumes are in [[KSTS volumes]], and the catalog entry is [[Abhinavagupta - Tantraloka (KSTS, vols I-XII)]].

The map was rebuilt on 6 Oct 2026 in phase 2 of the October 2026 plan. It replaces the earlier map, which had 228 anchor verses; the old note and TSV are in `.work/backup-phase2-2026-10-06/maps/`.

**How it was made:**
- Every page of the twelve volumes was read with Tesseract's Sanskrit model.
- Each verse's text was matched, in order, against the pages with fuzzy matching.
- The printed page numbers come from the running heads.
- Checking agents then read about 900 verses on the mapped page: the 175 structural repairs (174 Mūla blocks repaired, and 33.33, whose text was kept with a note line: phase 2 record, .work/plan/phase-2-state.json), the 686 verses of the reading check and 100 random verses. They found the page right in all but two cases, both now corrected (5.66 and 8.241).

**Reading the tables:**
- The PDF link opens the volume at the page.
- **Printed page with \***: no page number could be read on that page, so it is counted from the neighbouring pages.
- **? after the verses**: the verse's text matched the page only weakly (117 verses in all); look one page either side.
- A verse that runs onto the next page is listed only under the page where it starts.

**Pages missing from the scans:**
- Vol. X lacks one printed page at each of these places: pp. 69, 107, 145, 221, 259, 297, 335 and 373. The page before āhnika 19 (p. 182) is a blank.
  - On p. 259 the root text of 22.44 and the first half of 22.45 are lost; the map points both to the next page.
  - The verse notes themselves are complete, because they come from the e-text.
  - Another Digital Library of India scan of vol. X, `in.ernet.dli.2015.326661`, may fill these pages; it has not been checked.
- Vol. VIII lacks two pages between pp. 71 and 78.
- Vol. IX: the vault has the complete copy with handwritten notes. The clean Digital Library of India copy lacks pp. 217 and 255.

## Where the Mūla differs from the print

The verse notes' root text (Mūla) follows this edition, with three kinds of exception (Stephen's decisions, 6 Oct 2026).

1. **Evident misprints** (52 places). KSTS prints a non-word, or a form that breaks the grammar or metre; the commentary often quotes the right form. The Mūla gives the correct form.
2. **Bracketed readings.** KSTS prints the editor's suggested reading in brackets in 16 verses: square brackets in 12, round in 4. The Mūla copies them as printed. Seven brackets that only the Muktabodha e-text has were removed: 9.151, 17.23, 26.33, 26.43, 28.363, 31.84 and 32.50.
3. **33.33.** KSTS ends āhnika 33 at verse 32; the note says so.
